[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '      // One initialization per (launch state, user, resume target). Once the\r\n      // run is live, a repeat firing is a no-op rather than a reset.\r\n      const initKey = JSON.stringify([\r\n        state.mode,\r\n        userId ?? null,\r\n        classId,\r\n        guidedSessionKey ?? null,\r\n        genericResumeKey ?? null,\r\n        mcPracticePool?.slug ?? null,\r\n        initialIndexParam ?? null,\r\n      ]);\r\n      if (sessionInitKeyRef.current === initKey) return;\r\n      sessionInitKeyRef.current = initKey;\r\n\r\n      const mode = state.mode === \'practice-set\' ? \'type-drill\' : state.mode;\r\n      let questionQueue: string[] = [];\r\n      let rawQuestions: LRQuestion[] = [];\r\n\r\n      if (mode === \'adaptive\') {\r\n        rawQuestions = questionBank.getAllQuestions();\r\n      } else if (mode === \'full-section\' && state.config) {\r\n        const config = state.config as FullSectionConfig;\r\n        rawQuestions = questionBank.getSection(config.pt, config.section);\r\n        // An assigned set drawn from one section runs as that set, in the\r\n        // order it was assigned — not as every question in the section.\r\n        if (state.restrictQids?.length) {\r\n          const allowed = new Set(state.restrictQids);\r\n          const bySection = new Map(rawQuestions.map(q => [q.qid, q]));\r\n          const picked = state.restrictQids\r\n            .map(qid => bySection.get(qid))\r\n            .filter((q): q is LRQuestion => !!q);\r\n          if (picked.length > 0 && picked.length < rawQuestions.length) {\r\n            rawQuestions = picked;\r\n          } else if (picked.length > 0) {\r\n            rawQuestions = rawQuestions.filter(q => allowed.has(q.qid));\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        setHasTimer(config.timer.mode !== \'unlimited\');\r\n      } else if (mode === \'natural-drill\' && state.config) {\r\n        const config = state.config as TypeDrillConfig;\r\n        rawQuestions = config.selectedQids\r\n          ?.map(qid => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n          .filter((q): q is LRQuestion => !!q) ?? [];\r\n      } else if (mode === \'type-drill\' && state.config) {\r\n        const config = state.config as TypeDrillConfig;\r\n        \r\n        // Detect practice-set mode (Build-a-Set)\r\n        if (config.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0) {\r\n          setIsPracticeSetMode(true);\r\n          setHasTimer(true); // Guided drills use their configured countdown; other fixed queues use stopwatch.\r\n          rawQuestions = config.selectedQids\r\n            .map(qid => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n            .filter(Boolean) as LRQuestion[];\r\n        } else {\r\n          // Otherwise, filter by criteria\r\n          rawQuestions = questionBank.getQuestionsByFilter({\r\n            qtypes: config.qtypes.length > 0 ? config.qtypes : undefined,\r\n            difficulties: config.difficulties.length > 0 ? config.difficulties : undefined,\r\n            pts: config.pts.length > 0 ? config.pts : undefined,\r\n            sections: config.sections && config.sections.length > 0 ? config.sections : undefined,\r\n            qfeatures: config.qfeatures && config.qfeatures.length > 0 ? config.qfeatures : undefined,\r\n            reasoningTypes:\r\n              config.reasoningTypes && config.reasoningTypes.length > 0\r\n                ? config.reasoningTypes\r\n                : undefined,\r\n            searchQuery: config.searchQuery,\r\n            questionNumber: config.questionNumber,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Apply question pool filtering only if classId is available\r\n      let available = rawQuestions;\r\n      let exhausted = false;\r\n      \r\n      // A section is served whole or not at all. Pool dedupe used to run for\r\n      // every mode, so a full section silently lost any question the student\r\n      // had already met in a drill — PT103.S1 handed over 25 of its 26 with\r\n      // nothing on screen to say one was missing.\r\n      const fixedQueue = servesFixedQueue(\r\n        mode,\r\n        state.config as { selectedQids?: string[] } | null,\r\n      );\r\n\r\n      if (classId && !fixedQueue) {\r\n        // Dedupe read can fail (RLS/transport). Don\'t block the drill: fall back\r\n        // to an empty usage map (repeats possible) but tell the student why.\r\n        let usage: Map<string, QuestionUsage> = new Map();\r\n        try {\r\n          usage = await QuestionPoolService.getQuestionUsage(classId, mode);\r\n        } catch (err) {\r\n          console.error(\'Failed to load question history for dedupe:\', err);\r\n          toast.error("Couldn\'t check your question history; some questions may repeat.");\r\n        }\r\n        const typeDrillConfig = mode === \'type-drill\' ? (state.config as TypeDrillConfig) : null;\r\n        const poolSettings = {\r\n          allowRepeats: typeDrillConfig?.unseen_only ? false : settings.allowRepeats,\r\n          preferUnseen: typeDrillConfig?.unseen_only ? true : settings.preferUnseen,\r\n          recycleAfterDays: settings.recycleAfterDays\r\n        };\r\n\r\n        const filtered = QuestionPoolService.filterQuestionPool(\r\n          rawQuestions,\r\n          usage,\r\n          poolSettings\r\n        );\r\n        \r\n        available = filtered.available;\r\n        exhausted = filtered.exhausted;\r\n\r\n        setTotalPoolSize(rawQuestions.length);\r\n        setAvailablePoolSize(available.length);\r\n        setPoolExhausted(exhausted);\r\n        setPoolStatus(QuestionPoolService.getPoolStatus(rawQuestions.length, available.length, poolSettings));\r\n\r\n        if (exhausted) {\r\n          // Don\'t initialize session if pool is exhausted — surface the\r\n          // reason so the render layer can show a real message instead of\r\n          // the misleading "Session Complete / Loading…" card.\r\n          setBootError(\r\n            "You\'ve already practiced every available question for this drill type. Adjust your drill settings or come back after recycle."\r\n          );\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (rawQuestions.length === 0) {\r\n        setBootError(\r\n          "The question bank hasn\'t loaded any questions for this drill. Try refreshing or contact support if this persists."\r\n        );\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // For type-drill, apply balanced selection from available pool (only if not pre-selected)\r\n      let finalQuestions = available;\r\n      if (mode === \'type-drill\' && state.config) {\r\n        const config = state.config as TypeDrillConfig;\r\n        \r\n        // Skip pool filtering if questions were pre-selected\r\n        if (config.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0) {\r\n          finalQuestions = available; // Use them directly\r\n        } else {\r\n          // Balanced mix: group by type × level, round-robin select\r\n          const groups = new Map<string, LRQuestion[]>();\r\n          \r\n          for (const q of available) {\r\n            const key = `${q.qtype}-${q.difficulty}`;\r\n            if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, []);\r\n            groups.get(key)!.push(q);\r\n          }\r\n          \r\n          // Shuffle each group\r\n          for (const [, questions] of groups) {\r\n            for (let i = questions.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\r\n              const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\r\n              [questions[i], questions[j]] = [questions[j], questions[i]];\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n          \r\n          // Round-robin select\r\n          const selected: LRQuestion[] = [];\r\n          const groupArrays = Array.from(groups.values());\r\n          \r\n          while (selected.length < config.count && groupArrays.some(g => g.length > 0)) {\r\n            for (const group of groupArrays) {\r\n              if (group.length > 0 && selected.length < config.count) {\r\n                selected.push(group.shift()!);\r\n              }\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n          \r\n          // Final shuffle for variety\r\n          for (let i = selected.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\r\n            const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\r\n            [selected[i], selected[j]] = [selected[j], selected[i]];\r\n          }\r\n          \r\n          finalQuestions = selected;\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      questionQueue = finalQuestions.map(q => q.qid);\r\n\r\n      const requestedInitialIndex = initialIndexParam ? Number.parseInt(initialIndexParam, 10) : 0;\r\n      let initialIndex = Number.isFinite(requestedInitialIndex)\r\n        ? Math.min(Math.max(requestedInitialIndex, 0), Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0))\r\n        : 0;\r\n      let restoredAttempts: DrillSession[\'attempts\'] = new Map();\r\n      // Set only when a stored section had already run out of time.\r\n      let restoredRegulationMark: DrillSession[\'regulationMark\'] | undefined;\r\n          // only honest option; say so rather than silently dropping answers.\r\n          console.warn(\'[drill:mc-practice] stored resume set no longer matches pool, starting fresh\', {\r\n            slug: mcPracticePool.slug,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Guided drills resume the same way, and for the same reason: leaving a\r\n      // drill and coming back must return the student to the question they\r\n      // stopped on with their earlier answers intact, not to a fresh shuffle.\r\n      if (guidedSessionKey && user) {\r\n        const stored = await loadGuidedDrillSession(\r\n          user.id,\r\n          guidedSessionKey,\r\n          guidedLegacySessionKey,\r\n        );\r\n        if (isResumableGuidedSession(stored, questionQueue)) {\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (stored?.status === \'in_progress\') {\r\n          console.warn(\'[drill:guided] stored resume set no longer matches this drill, starting fresh\', {\r\n            key: guidedSessionKey,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Smart Drill, custom type drills and full sections. These are drawn\r\n      // fresh on every launch, so the stored queue IS the run: adopt it whole\r\n      // rather than comparing it to a new shuffle, or the student loses the\r\n      // questions they already answered. An explicit ?initialIndex wins, since\r\n      // that is someone deliberately jumping to a question.\r\n      if (genericResumeKey && user && !initialIndexParam) {\r\n        const stored = await loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n          user.id,\r\n          genericResumeKey,\r\n          genericLegacyResumeKey,\r\n        );\r\n        // A finished assignment\'s row is that sitting\'s record. This mount\r\n        // must never write over it: a reload during blind review re-mounts\r\n        // the runner with a fresh clock and no answers, and the first answer\r\n        // given on it used to flip the finished row back to in_progress.\r\n        if (assignmentId && stored?.status === \'completed\') {\r\n          genericFinalizedRef.current = true;\r\n        }\r\n        if (isResumableDrillSession(stored)) {\r\n          // Come back on the clock they left. A timed section that resumed\r\n          // with a fresh 35:00 was handing back time already spent, which\r\n          // makes the score it produces meaningless.\r\n          if (stored?.elapsed_ms) pendingResumeElapsedMs.current = stored.elapsed_ms;\r\n          if (stored?.regulation_answered_qids) {\r\n            restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored.regulation_answered_qids };\r\n          }\r\n          // A row stored at or after the buzzer has no clock left to resume\r\n          // on. It opens on the buzzer instead: answers kept, no time handed\r\n          // back, and the student is asked to finish and score the section.\r\n          const budget =\r\n            mode === \'full-section\'\r\n              ? sectionBudgetMs((state.config as FullSectionConfig | undefined)?.timer)\r\n              : undefined;\r\n          if (isSpentDrillSession(stored, budget)) {\r\n            pendingResumeAtBuzzer.current = true;\r\n            if (!restoredRegulationMark) {\r\n              restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored!.attempts.map((a) => a.qid) };\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n'}]

The stem is a better signal than a label\r\n  // the bank never agreed on — a bare "Except", a "Miscellaneous", or a\r\n  // sentence fragment that leaked in from the passage.\r\n  if (questionStem) {\r\n    const inferred = inferTypeFromStem(questionStem);\r\n    if (inferred !== \'Unknown\') {\r\n      return inferred;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  console.warn(`Unknown question type encountered: "${trimmed}"`);\r\n  return \'Unknown\';\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport class QuestionBank {\r\n  private questions: Map<string, LRQuestion> = new Map();\r\n  private manifest: QuestionManifest = {\r\n    totalQuestions: 0,\r\n    byPT: {},\r\n    byQType: {},\r\n    byFeature: {},\r\n    byReasoningType: {},\r\n    bySection: {},\r\n    byDifficulty: {},\r\n    sections: [],\r\n  };\r\n  private loadPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;\r\n\r\n  isReady(): boolean {\r\n    return this.questions.size > 0;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  async load(): Promise<void> {\r\n    if (this.isReady()) return;\r\n    if (this.loadPromise) return this.loadPromise;\r\n    this.loadPromise = this.doLoad().finally(() => {\r\n      if (!this.isReady()) this.loadPromise = null;\r\n    });\r\n    return this.loadPromise;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Fetch every section via a single merged bank file (built at `npm run build`\r\n  // by scripts/build-question-bank.mjs) so boot makes ONE request instead of\r\n  // ~156. Returns null if the merged file is absent or malformed so doLoad can\r\n  // fall back to the per-file path — the merged file is a pure optimization and\r\n  // can never be the sole source of the pool. COMPREHENSIVEAUDIT Phase 3.\r\n  private async fetchMergedBank(): Promise<Array<{ filename: string; raw: any[] }> | null> {\r\n    try {\r\n      const response = await fetch(\'/data/_bank.generated.json\');\r\n      if (!response.ok) return null;\r\n      const merged = await response.json();\r\n      if (!Array.isArray(merged) || merged.length === 0) return null;\r\n      const sections: Array<{ filename: string; raw: any[] }> = [];\r\n      for (const entry of merged) {\r\n        if (!entry || typeof entry.filename !== \'string\' || !Array.isArray(entry.questions)) {\r\n          console.error(\'Question bank: merged bank entry malformed; falling back to per-file load.\');\r\n          return null;\r\n        }\r\n        sections.push({ filename: entry.filename, raw: entry.questions });\r\n      }\r\n      return sections;\r\n    } catch {\r\n      // Missing file / parse error / offline: fall back to per-file fetches.\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private async fetchSectionsPerFile(): Promise<Array<{ filename: string; raw: any[] } | null>> {\r\n    // Fetch all section files in parallel — sequential awaits used to take\r\n    // 30-60s with ~190 files; parallel completes in well under a second.\r\n    return Promise.all(\r\n      JSON_FILES.map(async (filename) => {\r\n        try {\r\n          const response = await fetch(filename);\r\n          if (!response.ok) {\r\n            console.error(`Question bank: ${filename} returned HTTP ${response.status}`);\r\n            return null;\r\n          }\r\n          const raw = await response.json();\r\n          if (!Array.isArray(raw)) {\r\n            console.error(`Question bank: ${filename} is not an array`);\r\n            return null;\r\n          }\r\n          return { filename, raw };\r\n        } catch (err) {\r\n          console.error(`Failed to load ${filename}:`, err);\r\n          return null;\r\n        }\r\n      })\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * Which PTs have an authored sidecar, from the generated manifest.\r\n   *\r\n   * Returns null when the manifest is absent or unreadable, which means "ask\r\n   * for all of them" — the pre-manifest behaviour. A missing manifest must not\r\n   * be able to hide explanations that are actually there.\r\n   */\r\n  private async fetchExplanationManifest(): Promise<Set<number> | null> {\r\n    try {\r\n      const res = await fetch(\'/data/explanations/index.json\');\r\n      if (!res.ok) return null;\r\n      const json = await res.json();\r\n      if (!json || !Array.isArray(json.pts)) return null;\r\n      const pts = json.pts.filter((n: unknown): n is number => typeof n === \'number\');\r\n      return pts.length > 0 ? new Set<number>(pts) : null;\r\n    } catch {\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private async mergeExplanationSidecars(): Promise<void> {\r\n    const pts = new Set<number>();\r\n    for (const q of this.questions.values()) pts.add(q.pt);\r\n\r\n    // Only 21 of the bank\'s 59 PTs have an authored sidecar. Without the\r\n    // manifest this asked for all 59 and ate 38 x 404 on every cold load.\r\n    const authored = await this.fetchExplanationManifest();\r\n    const wanted = authored ? Array.from(pts).filter((pt) => authored.has(pt)) : Array.from(pts);\r\n\r\n    const results = await Promise.all(\r\n      wanted.map(async (pt) => {\r\n        try {\r\n          const res = await fetch(`/data/explanations/PT${pt}-explanations.json`);\r\n          if (!res.ok) return null;\r\n          const json = await res.json();\r\n          return json && typeof json === \'object\' ? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\r\n * full sections.\r\n *\r\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\r\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\r\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\r\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\r\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\r\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\r\n *\r\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\r\n * never collide:\r\n *\r\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug "drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>"\r\n *     homework       →  pool_slug "drill::assignment::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     everything else→  pool_slug "drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>"\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport {\r\n  loadPracticeSession,\r\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n  completePracticeSession,\r\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\r\nimport type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n\r\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\r\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeIdentity = {\r\n  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\r\n  day: number;\r\n  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\r\n};\r\n\r\n/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\r\nfunction fingerprint(input: string): string {\r\n  let h = 2166136261;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\r\n  }\r\n  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Key one run\'s resume row.\r\n *\r\n * A homework assignment is identified by the assignment: two assignments of\r\n * the same section are two sittings, and a row named by the section alone let\r\n * the second one open on the first one\'s answers and the first one\'s spent\r\n * clock. Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\r\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\r\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. Returns null for\r\n * the surfaces that own their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for\r\n * anything launched from an explicit list of qids, where the caller already\r\n * knows the set.\r\n */\r\nexport function drillResumeKey(input: {\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  config?: unknown;\r\n  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\r\n  /** The classroom homework assignment this run belongs to, when it does. */\r\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\r\n}): string | null {\r\n  if (input.assignmentId) {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}assignment::${input.assignmentId}`;\r\n  }\r\n  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const mode = input.mode;\r\n  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\r\n\r\n  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const pt = config.pt;\r\n    const section = config.section;\r\n    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\r\n    if (selected?.length) {\r\n      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\r\n      // same run, a different list is a different one.\r\n      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\r\n    }\r\n    const shape = JSON.stringify({\r\n      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\r\n      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\r\n      pts: config.pts ?? [],\r\n      sections: config.sections ?? [],\r\n      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\r\n      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\r\n      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\r\n      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\r\n      count: config.count ?? null,\r\n    });\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\r\nexport async function loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return null;\r\n  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\r\n  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\r\n  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */\r\nexport function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\r\n * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\r\n * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\r\n */\r\nexport function completeDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Is this stored row worth resuming?\r\n *\r\n * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\r\n * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\r\n * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered\r\n * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only\r\n * freeze a stale shuffle in place.\r\n */\r\nexport function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\r\n  return (\r\n    !!stored &&\r\n    stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n    stored.attempts.length > 0\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Was this row\'s section clock already spent when it was stored?\r\n *\r\n * A row written at or after the buzzer carries elapsed_ms equal to the budget\r\n * (the countdown reports zero, so the spend is the whole budget). Restoring it\r\n * as an ordinary resume opens the section on 00:00 with no buzzer, because the\r\n * regulation mark it also carries tells the buzzer effect it has already\r\n * asked. The runner uses this to open such a row on the buzzer instead: the\r\n * answers are kept, the clock is not handed back, and the student is asked to\r\n * finish and score the section as it was sat.\r\n *\r\n * Untimed runs have no budget and are never "spent".\r\n */\r\nexport function isSpentDrillSession(\r\n  stored: DrillResumeSession | null,\r\n  budgetMs: number | undefined,\r\n): boolean {\r\n  if (!stored || !budgetMs || !Number.isFinite(budgetMs)) return false;\r\n\r\n      // Intro to LR practice sets resume from durable storage, not from the URL.\r\n      // A student who closed the tab, signed out, or moved to another device\r\n      // comes back to the question they stopped on with every prior answer\r\n      // still marked answered in the navigator.\r\n      if (isMcPractice && mcPracticePool && user) {\r\n        const stored = await loadPracticeSession(user.id, mcPracticePool.slug);\r\n        const sameSet =\r\n          !!stored &&\r\n          stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n          stored.question_queue.length === questionQueue.length &&\r\n          stored.question_queue.every((qid) => questionQueue.includes(qid));\r\n\r\n        if (stored && sameSet) {\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = Math.min(\r\n            Math.max(stored.current_index, 0),\r\n            Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (stored && !sameSet && stored.status === \'in_progress\') {\r\n          // The frozen pool changed under a saved run. Starting clean is the\r\n          // only honest option; say so rather than silently dropping answers.\r\n          console.warn(\'[drill:mc-practice] stored resume set no longer matches pool, starting fresh\', {\r\n            slug: mcPracticePool.slug,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Guided drills resume the same way, and for the same reason: leaving a\r\n      // drill and coming back must return the student to the question they\r\n'}]
